### Пересменка: archive detail contract остановлен как bounded node, 06.09.2026 04:41 МСК

Новый `shift-log-detail-readonly` сначала подтвердил пустой active list, затем проверил существующий archive: **12 видимых карточек**, detail каждой возвращает **409 `Запись пересменки не найдена.`** одинаково на desktop/360/390/430. - Root cause: `ShiftLogService.archive()`/`listLogs()` разрешают `isDeleted` через `archive:'true'`, а `getLog()` применяет `logWhere(...includeClosed:'true')` без archive и снова требует `isDeleted:false`. Видимые archived rows поэтому не могут открыться. Обычный UI также показывает повторяющиеся diagnostic titles и английский fixture text; archive visibility/provenance должен быть согласован с detail authority.

### UI-SWEEP-036 bounded pre-change checkpoint, 2026-09-12

- Continued the existing Goal repository-first without reopening discovery. Current generated evidence confirms 284 reconciled surfaces, 957 source controls and 954 exact bindings (99.69%); the three remaining interaction rows are the single `UI-SWEEP-063` cause. Current matrices report interaction 954 PASS / 3 FAIL, Back 1614 PASS / 4 FAIL / 1 PHYSICAL_PENDING and text runtime scan 2711 PASS / 35 retained FAIL rows. The canonical bounded severity declaration remains P0 = 0, P1 = 1, P2 = 10; installed-PWA/camera/microphone/physical Android Back remain PENDING.
- Re-read the exact current register entry for `UI-SWEEP-036`: `Пересменка / Архив` lists soft-archived rows because `archive()` calls `listLogs(..., archive:'true')`, while `getLog()`, `markRead()`, `reads()` and every mutation visibility check call `logWhere(..., includeClosed:'true')` without archive and therefore require `isDeleted:false`. The reproduced 409 at desktop/360/390/430 is still factual and the retained BEFORE/review evidence remains immutable.
- Change-impact boundary is broader than one predicate. `GET /shift-log/archive` is guarded by `shift-log.archive.read|shift-log.manage`, while ordinary `GET /shift-log/:id` and `POST /shift-log/:id/read` require `shift-log.read`; the current detail UI also exposes comment/file/important-close actions without an archive-readonly branch. A naive `archive:true` pass-through would change backend authority for deleted rows and could expose mutation paths; changing the ordinary detail guard could either deny valid active readers or grant archive readers too much. Factory and department constraints, blocked/deleted user denial and UserFactoryAccess remain mandatory.
- Existing owners confirmed: `backend/src/modules/shift-log/shift-log.controller.ts`, `backend/src/modules/shift-log/shift-log.service.ts`, `frontend/src/screens/ShiftLogScreen.tsx`, shared `PermissionGuard`/`UserContextService`, plus the separate guarded `ArchiveService` read model. No schema or migration is indicated. The exact bounded test must cover active detail compatibility, archive-only detail/read semantics, mutation denial, factory/department/cross-factory denial, blocked/deleted access and desktop/360/390/430 UI/Back/text outcomes.
- Stopped before the risky product change under the repository model-escalation rule. Recommend GPT-6 Astra High for the permission-boundary design and proof; Astra Medium is acceptable only for a narrowly pre-agreed archive-detail endpoint contract. `UI-SWEEP-063` was not entered because the requested order requires closing 036 first. No product source, backend/API/schema/DB/data, `.env`, uploads, screenshots, manifests, ZIPs or runtime processes changed in this checkpoint; matrices were inspected but not rewritten because coverage did not change.
- Additional bounded contract evidence: `shift-log.archive.read` is an independent capability, granted by default to MANAGEMENT/ADMIN, while many operational roles receive only `shift-log.read`; MASTER reaches the archive through `shift-log.manage`. Historical P8 evidence explicitly requires the soft-archived `Проверка пересменки для пилота` record to remain preserved in the archive while disappearing from the operational list. Therefore the fix cannot collapse archive detail into the ordinary `shift-log.read` endpoint or hide soft-archived rows. The least-privilege design must keep an archive-specific guarded read path, reuse `logWhere` factory/department scope, return a read-only archive presentation, and leave `PATCH`, comment, attachment upload, important-close and archive commands on the existing non-deleted visibility predicate. Existing `stage14-shift-log-regression.js` does not prove archived detail or its denial matrix; those are required additive targeted assertions before acceptance.
